## Best date plans

### 2026-11-12 — Sofreh + Bela Lugosi Meets a Brooklyn Gorilla — Dave Abramson introduction

**Dinner, then Bela Lugosi Meets a Brooklyn Gorilla — Dave Abramson introduction.** Persian at Sofreh. It is 1.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Bela Lugosi Meets a Brooklyn Gorilla — Dave Abramson introduction starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.

#### [Accent Sisters × MOCA: Taiwanese Hokkien, Hakka & Teochew Dialect Corner](https://www.mocanyc.org/event/accent-sisters-dialect-cornertaiwanese-hokkien-hakka-teochew/)

4:00 PM · museums · Museum of Chinese in America · Chinatown · Manhattan · Score 57.0  
Reserve Tickets Become a Member 潮州话、客家话、台语方言角来啦! 🍜 别人搬家是背井离乡，客家人搬家是继承家业；潮汕人搬到哪里，都坚持小孩必须说潮汕话。这次重音方言角 × 纽约美国华人博物馆 MOCA，我们请来四位嘉宾，聊聊台语，潮州话，粤语，和客家话这几个默默称霸海外的方言。我们会一起探索方言里的音调音律，窥探豆腐的历史前生，听听周敦颐第三十六代传人的家族传奇，再跟着潮州博主看看潮汕人在全球扎根的轶事。本次活动我们请来了四位嘉宾: 李岳燁 Dennis：导演、剧作家,也是个夜店咖,常驻DJ台正前方。矿工爺爺带大,家里没人会念「燁」,干脆叫他「华」。曾因为疏於讲台语被外婆训过。 周晓蓉：周敦颐第三十六代传人,汕头金中校友会秘书长。祖先爱莲「出淤泥而不染」,她爱越野,专往泥里钻。 陈洁莹:「潮汕人在纽约」主理人,韩江边长大的野孩子。坚信小孩必须说潮汕话,家里…

## Where to eat

NYC dining for November 11, 2026: lunch specials, oyster hours, recent openings and Thanksgiving meals. Check ordering deadlines; listed offers do not guarantee remaining seats or inventory.

Dining research: 2026-11-11T10:40:42+00:00

No Michelin award claims are added, and all recognition fields remain empty. Michelin listings were not independently checked in this review. Eater heatmap inclusion and a chef's previous employers do not establish Michelin stars or guide selection for these restaurants.

### The Dynamo Room: $35.95 express lunch and daily happy hour

Deals & happy hours — The Dynamo Room

Express lunch includes rolls, a salad and a choice of pasta or salmon. Happy hour offers oysters, sliders and a martini-salad-fries combination.

Price: Express lunch $35.95; steak frites supplement $10. Happy hour: 3 oysters $9.95, 6 $18.95, 12 $36.95; Miller High Life $6.95; wine $12.95; mini dirty martini $11.95; Dynamo Trio $29.95.  
Schedule: Lunch Monday–Thursday 11:30am–2:30pm, combining menu times with official opening days. Happy hour daily 4–6pm.  
Conditions: Happy hour only at the bar or terrace. Trio includes a mini dirty martini, Caesar salad and beef-fat fries. Minimum purchases and reservation requirements are not stated. Tax, gratuity and service charges are unspecified. Holiday exceptions unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://v4-thedynamoroom.getbento.com/menus/)

### Junoon: $43 two-course or $49 three-course lunch

Deals & happy hours — Junoon

The lunch Prestige menu includes basmati rice and naan, with vegetarian choices alongside chicken, seafood and goat.

Price: Two courses $43; three courses $49.  
Schedule: Lunch; exact days and hours unconfirmed.  
Conditions: The menu states a 20% service charge for parties of five or more. Other tax and gratuity treatment is unspecified. Lunch minimum-party and whole-table requirements are not stated; the dinner participation rule is not assumed to apply. Booking availability and holiday hours unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)

### The Dutch: $2 oysters daily, 3–5pm

Deals & happy hours — The Dutch

A daily two-hour oyster offer provides a lower-cost way to visit this Soho restaurant.

Price: $2 per oyster.  
Schedule: Every day, 3–5pm.  
Conditions: Minimum quantity, required accompanying purchases, seating restrictions and reservation requirements are not stated. Tax, gratuity and service charges are unspecified. Holiday exceptions unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.thedutchnyc.com/menus/)

### Frenzie: late-night pizza-and-drink packages from $40

Deals & happy hours — Frenzie

The Confidant team's wine-and-pizza restaurant offers a cheese pie bundled with beer or two cocktails after 10pm.

Price: Cheese pie plus Narragansett pitcher $40; plus two daiquiris $45; plus two martinis $50. Pepperoni supplement $4.  
Schedule: Thursday–Saturday after 10pm.  
Conditions: Walk-in only. Menu subject to seasonal changes. Do not assume the packages are available for takeout; the separate takeout policy is Monday–Thursday, in person and subject to kitchen capacity. Tax, tip and service charges unspecified. Holiday exceptions unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.frenzienyc.com/menus/)

### Amélie West Village: $26 lunch, $1.50 oysters and $26 early-bird meal

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

Choose a two-course weekday lunch or visit later for oyster and wine-flight specials. The early-bird offer pairs a burger or mussels with fries and a beer or glass of wine.

Price: Lunch $26; lunch burger supplement $5, steak or Niçoise supplement $8. Happy-hour oysters $1.50 each; three-wine flight $14; selected cocktails $10; early-bird meal $26.  
Schedule: Lunch Monday–Friday noon–3:30pm. Happy-hour food and early-bird menu daily 4–6pm; drink menu says opening until 6pm.  
Conditions: Lunch excludes tax and gratuity; one bread basket included, additional basket $3. Happy-hour fees, minimum purchases and seating restrictions are not stated. Reservations offered, not stated as mandatory. Contact page summarizes happy hour as 4–6pm, while the drink menu allows earlier drinks; confirm if visiting before 4pm. Holiday exceptions unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/happy-hour)

### Edy's Grocer: $75 Korean–Lebanese hot pot with Jamesy

Pop-ups — Edy's Grocer

A guest-chef dinner combines kimchi-vegetable and sumac-beef-bone broths with selectable hot-pot ingredients, opening bites and a persimmon-labneh dessert.

Price: $75 per person; Lebanese beer and wine à la carte.  
Schedule: Monday, November 16; seatings at 6pm and 8pm.  
Conditions: One ticket equals one seat. Limited seating, no substitutions, all ticket sales final. Tax, gratuity and service charges are unspecified. Listing was not marked sold out, but live seat inventory was not verified.  
Event date: 2026-11-16  
[Source](https://edysgrocer.com/shop/p/dinner-at-the-grocer-jamesy)

Special menus — La Compagnie Flatiron

Duchêne joins as consulting chef with seasonal French-inspired menus at La Compagnie Flatiron, its Centre Street sibling and Experimental Cocktail Club. This is an ongoing menu collaboration, not a one-night pop-up.

Schedule: Announced launch Sunday, November 8. Flatiron: Monday–Wednesday 5pm–midnight; Thursday–Saturday 5pm–1am; Sunday 5–11pm.  
Conditions: Ongoing collaboration, not a one-night pop-up. No end date stated. The official page links wine lists, not a readable food menu for this collaboration; individual food prices remain unverified. Booking inventory and holiday exceptions unconfirmed.  
From: 2026-11-08  
[Source](https://www.experimentalgroup.com/new-york/la-compagnie-flatiron)

Schedule: Preorder by November 20 at 5pm; pickup November 24 or 25. Pickup hours unspecified.  
Conditions: Turkey is not included. Choose chestnut-andouille or apple-sage stuffing. Heating instructions supplied. Taxes, service fees and order inventory unconfirmed. Listed start/end dates refer to pickup, not the ordering window.

Through: 2026-11-25

### Lord's: Thanksgiving takeaway order deadline November 12

Special menus — Lord's

The reported package combines roast turkey breast, a braised-turkey-leg and black-truffle pot pie, stuffing, pumpkin pie and next-day sandwich ingredients.

Price: $200 for two; $400 for four  
Schedule: Order by November 12; pickup November 26, 9am–1pm  
Conditions: Advance preorder required. Official ordering storefront returned a JavaScript shell, so remaining availability, fees, tax, gratuity and cancellation terms could not be verified. No Thanksgiving dine-in availability is implied.  
Event date: 2026-11-26  
Through: 2026-11-12  
Published: 2026-11-10

Contemporary Mexican · $$$$ · NoHo · Manhattan · Score 65.0  
Enrique Olvera's new restaurant in the former Atla space focuses on freshly pressed tortillas and comal cooking with chef Milton Revilla. Eater confirms the November 10 opening. A nearby dinner option for CultureHub; the reported $135 prix fixe is a splurge, with a la carte options also described.

## Book ahead

#### [Two Strangers (Carry a Cake Across New York) — final Broadway performance](https://twostrangersmusical.com/)

2026-11-29 · 3:00 PM · theater · Longacre Theatre · Theater District · Manhattan · Score 65.0  
Sam Tutty and Christiani Pitts star in a two-person musical comedy about an optimistic British visitor and a practical New Yorker navigating wedding errands, family secrets and unexpected connection.
**Timing:** Playbill lists the final Sunday performance at 3pm; running time is 2 hours 15 minutes including intermission.
**Practical note:** The production's official site confirms the closing. Current ticket prices and seat inventory were not verified.
**Why now:** This is an announced Broadway closing, not simply the last date currently displayed in a ticket calendar.
**Book soon:** The official production website says MUST CLOSE NOVEMBER 29 and advertises only three weeks left.
[Official](https://twostrangersmusical.com/) · [Tickets / booking](https://www.telecharge.com/Two-Strangers-Carry-a-Cake-Across-New-York-tickets) · [Discovered via Playbill Productions](https://playbill.com/production/two-strangers-carry-a-cake-across-new-york-broadway-longacre-theatre-2025)
**Sale/deadline:** 2026-11-29

#### [Elektra — closing performance after multiple sold-out dates](https://tfana.org/events/elektra-2026-11-11-730-pm)

2026-12-06 · 7:30 PM · theater · Polonsky Shakespeare Center · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 64.0  
Daniel Fish directs Sophokles' tragedy in Anne Carson's translation, with choral music by Ted Hearne. Tina Benko plays Elektra in a production exploring grief, revenge and their consequences.
**Timing:** The official calendar labels December 6 at 7:30pm as the closing performance.
**Practical note:** The calendar marks November 18–21 and the November 22 matinee sold out. Later dates have seat-selection links, but individual seats were not inspected.
**Why now:** Documented sold-out dates provide a concrete reason to select a remaining performance before the confirmed closing.
**Book soon:** The theater explicitly identifies December 6 at 7:30pm as the closing performance, and multiple earlier performances are already marked sold out.
[Official](https://tfana.org/events/elektra-2026-11-11-730-pm) · [Tickets / booking](https://tfana.org/events/elektra-2026-12-06-730-pm/seats)
**Sale/deadline:** 2026-12-06

#### [The Imaginary Invalid — final weeks](https://www.roundabouttheatre.org/production/2026-2027-season/the-imaginary-invalid/)

2026-11-22 · Time to confirm · theater · Todd Haimes Theatre · Theater District · Manhattan · Score 62.0  
Bill Irwin stars in his adaptation of Molière's comedy about a hypochondriac scheming to marry his daughter to a doctor. Brandon J. Dirden directs an ensemble including Rachel Bloom, Brooks Ashmanskas and Dylan Baker.
**Timing:** November 22 is the confirmed closing date; final performance time was not verified.
**Practical note:** Runs 90 minutes without intermission and uses theatrical haze. The ticket seller returned a queue page, so prices and remaining seats could not be verified.
**Why now:** The official closing falls in eleven days, just beyond the weekly event window.
**Book soon:** Roundabout explicitly advertises FINAL WEEKS and a September 25–November 22 run. The alert is the confirmed closing, not presumed demand.

**Sale/deadline:** 2026-11-22

#### [Tuomas A. Laitinen: Siphon — closing November 22](https://pioneerworks.org/exhibitions/tuomas-a-laitinen-siphon)

2026-11-22 · 12:00 PM · art · Pioneer Works · Red Hook · Brooklyn · Score 62.0  
An immersive installation combines octopus imagery, blown-glass sculptures, Kosen Ohtsubo's ikebana arrangements and directional sound featuring Nyokabi Kariuki. Developed through research into octopus intelligence, sleep and consciousness.
**Timing:** Final exhibition day; regular Sunday gallery hours are noon–6pm.
**Practical note:** Galleries are open Wednesday–Sunday, noon–6pm. The building has an elevator and accessible restrooms.
**Why now:** The last viewing dates fall immediately beyond the seven-day discovery window.
**Book soon:** Pioneer Works confirms a November 22 closing, placing the exhibition in its final two weeks. This is a visit deadline, not a ticket-scarcity claim.
[Official](https://pioneerworks.org/exhibitions/tuomas-a-laitinen-siphon) · [Tickets / booking](https://pioneerworks.org/exhibitions/tuomas-a-laitinen-siphon) · [Discovered via Hyperallergic](https://hyperallergic.com/before-ai-there-was-the-octopus/)
**Sale/deadline:** 2026-11-22

#### [I Became We — final performance](https://atlantictheater.org/production/i-became-we/)

2026-11-22 · 2:00 PM · theater · Linda Gross Theater · Chelsea · Manhattan · Score 55.0  
Mona Pirnot's first-person monologue is performed by a sixteen-person choir, with music by Jack D. Coen and direction by Sarah Benson. The world premiere explores a woman in her mid-thirties confronting a consequential personal decision.
**Timing:** Final performance at 2pm; running time is 70 minutes without intermission.
**Practical note:** Open captions are provided at every performance. Published ticket reservations are non-refundable.
**Why now:** Only eleven days remain before this production's confirmed ending.
**Book soon:** Atlantic confirms the run ends November 22 and lists its final performance at 2pm. The final-week calendar still provides public ticket links; availability can change.

Clare Barron's world-premiere play, directed by Anne Kauffman, explores a thirty-year affair and its consequences. The cast includes Alison Pill, Sydney Lemmon and Ato Blankson-Wood.
